Method: 提出控制Laguerre镶嵌（CLT）方法，基于地面成本满足扭曲条件时最优输运映射的Laguerre镶嵌结构，将其推广到受控智能体的最优运动成本场景。

Result: 给出了两种线性受控智能体（最小能量和最小时间目标）下的CLT实例，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 建立了最优控制与最优输运之间的桥梁，为受约束智能体的群体运动规划提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: We study the optimal transport of optimally controlled agents from a compactly supported absolutely continuous source to a discrete target measure. The ground cost for the transport is induced by the optimal cost of the agents' motion. When this ground cost satisfies the twist condition, the optimal transport map is given almost everywhere in terms of a Laguerre tessellation of the state space. We refer to this control-theoretic generalization of Laguerre tessellation as Control Laguerre Tessellation (CLT), and illustrate it for two ground costs induced by linear controlled agents with minimum energy and minimum time objectives.

</details>


### [113] [Polyhedral extended formulations that approximate the Gomory closure for packing problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09222)
*Friedrich Eisenbrand,Samuel Fiorini,Lars Rohwedder,Jiaye Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于第一原理的多面体多项式扩展公式，用于逼近0/1背包问题的Gomory闭包，近似因子为1+ε；同时利用通信复杂性方法得到了在某些参数范围内更优的拟多项式多面体扩展公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0/1背包问题的线性规划松弛可以通过Gomory割平面进行收紧，但Gomory闭包上的优化问题是NP困难的。Mastrolilli (2020) 使用Lasserre层级得到了非多面体的凸扩展公式，本文旨在构造一个多面体且多项式的扩展公式来达到相同的近似保证。

Method: 基于第一原理构造多面体多项式扩展公式，该构造也适用于更高阶的迭代P^{(t)}。同时，利用通信复杂性方法，通过扩展Yannakakis协议来判断Alice的团与Bob的稳定集是否相交，从而得到拟多项式多面体扩展公式。

Result: 成功构造了多面体多项式扩展公式，在固定ε>0时以1+ε因子逼近Gomory闭包P'；通信复杂性方法得到了拟多项式多面体扩展公式，在某些参数范围内表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文提供了两种构造方法来近似Gomory闭包：一种基于第一原理的多面体多项式扩展公式，另一种基于通信复杂性的拟多项式扩展公式，后者在特定参数下具有优势。

Abstract: We consider $0/1$ packing problems $\max\{c^T x \colon Ax \leq 1, \, x \in \{0,1\}^n\}$, with $A \in \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{m \times n}$. A way to solve such problems is via tightening the linear programming relaxation $P$ with Gomory \emph{cutting-planes}. The Gomory-closure $P'$ of $P$ is the intersection of $P$ with all its cutting planes. The optimization problem over $P'$ is NP-hard. Mastrolilli (2020) has shown that for fixed $ε>0$, the Lasserre hierarchy yields a polynomial-size convex but non-polyhedral extended formulation that approximates $P'$ up to a factor of $1+ε$. Our main result is the construction of a polyhedral and polynomial extended formulation that approximates $P'$ with the same approximation guarantee. Our construction is based on first principles. Like Mastrolilli's approach, ours also applies to higher iterates $P^{(t)}$ for fixed $t$ and $ε>0$.
  In contrast to an explicit construction, communication complexity provides an alternative way to describe extended formulations. Using this approach we obtain a quasi-polynomial polyhedral extended formulation for the above problem that is superior in some parameter regimes. To achieve this, we describe a communication protocol extending Yannakakis' protocol to decide whether the clique of Alice and the stable set of Bob intersect.

</details>


### [114] [A general framework for nonlocal traffic flow models on networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09227)
*Alexander Keimer,Lukas Pflug,Florian Prohaska*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算框架，用于多商品宏观非局部交通流网络建模，通过非局部速度函数和缓冲区交汇点动力学耦合标量守恒律，并实现了动态k-最短路路由与优化路由策略，数值实验验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的宏观交通流模型在多商品网络场景下缺乏统一的计算框架，特别是非局部相互作用和路由决策与交通演化的反馈机制尚未被有效建模。

Method: 基于标量守恒律描述道路上的交通演化，引入非局部速度函数；通过缓冲区动力学实现交汇点处的耦合；采用动态k-最短路路由（基于实时行程时间和等待时间）以及时间窗口内总行程时间最小化的优化路由作为示例。

Result: 从小型测试到大型网格网络的多组数值实验表明，该框架具有鲁棒性，并能有效对比不同路由策略的表现。

Conclusion: 该框架自然地建立了交通演化与路径选择之间的反馈回路，为多商品非局部交通流网络的建模与路由优化提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: We present a general computational framework for macroscopic nonlocal traffic flow models on networks with multiple commodities. The model combines scalar conservation laws on edges with nonlocal velocity functions and couples them via buffer-based junction dynamics.
  Routing is defined in general as a prescription for distributing drivers across outgoing road segments. As an example, we implement dynamic k-shortest-path routing, where travel times along roads and waiting times at intersections are used to compute shortest paths to the commodities' destinations at each time step, and drivers are distributed accordingly. In another example, we optimize routing over a considered time horizon to minimize the total travel time. This framework naturally creates a feedback loop between traffic evolution and route choice.
  Numerical examples, ranging from small test cases to large grid-like networks, demonstrate the robustness of the approach and allow for a comparison of different routing strategies.

</details>


### [115] [On feasibility problems with spectral constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09254)
*Shravan Mohan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究矩阵可行性问题"找到X in K ∩ C"，其中K是闭凸矩阵集，C由奇异值凸约束定义。利用谱传递恒等式，投影到C简化为SVD加小型二次规划。对七种自然多面体S，在交替投影循环中嵌入该投影算子。在线性约束和椭球约束两类K上实验，揭示了快速可行、慢速尾收敛或信息性不可行平台三种状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有奇异值凸约束的矩阵可行性问题，这类问题在机器学习、信号处理和控制理论中有广泛应用，但缺乏高效的通用求解方法。

Method: 利用谱传递恒等式将投影到奇异值约束集C简化为SVD加小型二次规划；对七种自然多面体S，在交替投影循环中嵌入该投影算子；在线性约束（仿射子空间∩逐元素箱）和椭球约束（非中心各向异性Frobenius椭球）两类K上实验。

Result: 实验揭示三种收敛状态：快速可行（问题可行时快速收敛）、慢速尾收敛（接近可行时收敛变慢）、信息性不可行平台（不可行时投影误差稳定在某个水平）。

Conclusion: 提出的交替投影方法能有效处理带有奇异值凸约束的矩阵可行性问题，且能通过收敛行为区分问题的可解性状态。

Abstract: We study matrix feasibility problems "find X in K intersect C" where K is a closed convex matrix set and C = {X : sigma(X) in S} is defined by a convex constraint S on the ordered singular values. Using the classical spectral transfer identity, projection onto C reduces to an SVD plus a small quadratic program. For seven natural polyhedral S we embed this projector in a plain alternating projection (AP) loop. We experiment with two concrete families of K - linear constraints (an affine subspace intersected with an entrywise box) and ellipsoidal constraints (a non-centered anisotropic Frobenius ellipsoid) - although the method applies equally to more general convex constraints. The experiments expose three regimes: rapid feasibility, slow tail convergence, or informative infeasibility plateaus.

</details>


### [116] [On Incremental Design of Large-Scale Networks of Nondeterministic Metric Finite State Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09313)
*Giordano Pola*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非确定性和度量有限状态系统网络（NoS）的增量式控制设计方法，通过提取原始NoS的递增序列并逐步求解，为每个被控对象设计局部控制器，以在期望精度内强制执行由正则语言描述的局部规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决NoS中局部控制器设计问题，该架构扩展了分散控制架构，需要在非确定性和度量有限状态系统组成的网络中，为被控对象设计控制器，使其满足给定精度的局部规范。

Method: 采用增量式方法，在原始NoS中提取一个递增的NoS序列，并在该序列上逐步求解控制器设计问题。

Result: 能够在递增的子系统序列上找到满足局部规范的控制器解。

Conclusion: 增量式方法能有效解决NoS中带精度要求的局部控制器设计问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we address control design of Networks of nondeterministic and metric finite state Systems (NoS) where some systems are plants, others act as controllers, and some are uncontrolled processes. The control architecture of NoS extends decentralized control architectures. The problem addressed consists in designing local controllers for enforcing on the corresponding plants, local specifications given by regular languages over plants' state alphabet, up to desired accuracies. The approach used to solve the problem is termed incremental and consists in finding solutions on an increasing sequence of NoS extracted from the original NoS.

</details>


### [117] [Convex Relaxations for the Optimization of Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09423)
*Hongyi Zhang,Yuehaw Khoo,Tianyun Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对高维状态空间中马尔可夫过程优化（在给定成本下插值两个概率分布）的维数灾难问题，提出了基于顺序耦合的凸松弛方法，利用局部边缘和簇矩提供可计算下界，并恢复低阶统计量。该方法将动态最优输运作为特例，并推广到更一般的马尔可夫过程（如伊辛模型间的约束过程）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维状态空间中，在两个指定概率分布间插值的马尔可夫过程优化面临维数灾难，完整分布表示不可行，需要可计算的近似方法。

Method: 将问题重构为顺序耦合形式，基于局部边缘和簇矩开发凸松弛，利用问题的局部性和稀疏交互结构。

Result: 提供了可计算的下界，能够恢复中间定律的低阶统计量；识别动态最优输运为特例并恢复Benamou-Brenier动力学；方法推广到伊辛模型间的约束过程。

Conclusion: 提出的凸松弛方法有效缓解了高维马尔可夫过程优化中的维数灾难，具有通用性和可计算性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of optimizing Markov processes that interpolate between two prescribed probability distributions while minimizing a given cost. The main computational challenge is the curse of dimensionality: in high-dimensional state spaces, representing the full distribution is intractable. To address this, we reformulate the problem in terms of sequential couplings and develop convex relaxations based on local marginals and cluster moments. These relaxations exploit locality and sparse interaction structure, provide computable lower bounds, and recover low-order statistics of the intermediate laws. We identify dynamic optimal transport as a special case of our Markov process optimization problem and develop a procedure for recovering the underlying Benamou--Brenier dynamics from the relaxed solution. We also show that the procedure extends to more general Markov processes and illustrate it with a constrained process between Ising models.

</details>


### [118] [The Continuous Relaxation of Sparse PCA is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09429)
*Linbin Li,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明，在同时满足L1范数不等式和L2范数等式约束下最大化对称二次型（SPCA的连续松弛形式）是NP-hard问题，其变体（L1和L2均为不等式约束）同样为NP-hard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏主成分分析（SPCA）的连续松弛形式被广泛使用，但其计算复杂度尚未被严格证明。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，明确该问题的计算复杂性边界。

Method: 通过理论归约证明，将所提优化问题归约到已知的NP-hard问题，从而证明其NP-hard性质。

Result: 证明标准SPCA连续松弛形式（L1不等式+L2等式约束）是NP-hard的；同时证明其变体（L1和L2均为不等式约束）也是NP-hard的。

Conclusion: SPCA的连续松弛形式本质上是计算困难的，这为理解其近似算法的必要性和设计更高效的求解策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Maximizing a symmetric quadratic form under simultaneous L1 norm inequality and L2 norm equality constraints is a standard and widely used continuous relaxation for Sparse Principal Component Analysis (SPCA). This paper settles the computational complexity of this continuous formulation by proving it is NP-hard. Furthermore, the variant with both L1 and L2 norm inequalities is also shown to be NP-hard.

</details>


### [119] [Inertial forward-backward algorithm with exterior penalization and Tikhonov regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09495)
*Siqi Qu,Juan Peypouquet,Mathias Staudigl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在实Hilbert空间中提出了一种多尺度惯性前向-后向分裂算法，结合多尺度惩罚和消失Tikhonov正则化，用于求解带约束的单调包含问题，并在温和约束规格条件下证明了迭代序列强收敛到最小范数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理带约束变分不等式时，通常只能保证弱收敛性，难以获得最小范数解。本文旨在通过引入离散Tikhonov中心路径和多尺度惯性技术，突破这一限制，实现强收敛到最小范数解。

Method: 提出多尺度惯性前向-后向分裂算法，融合多尺度惩罚算子（涵盖光滑、非光滑及混合类型）与消失Tikhonov正则化，并引入离散Tikhonov中心路径理论进行分析。

Result: 在一般框架下建立了迭代序列的弱收敛性；进一步在温和约束规格条件下，证明了算法强收敛到约束单调包含问题的最小范数解。

Conclusion: 本文为求解带约束变分不等式的最小范数解提供了一套统一的数值分裂框架，兼具理论保证与算法实用性。

Abstract: In a real Hilbertian setting, we develop in this paper numerical splitting techniques guaranteeing strong convergence to the least norm solution of constrained variational inequalities. We develop a multiscale inertial forward-backward splitting algorithm for solving constrained monotone inclusion problems with multiscale penalization and vanishing Tikhonov regularization. The proposed framework accommodates smooth, nonsmooth, and mixed smooth--nonsmooth penalty operators, providing a unified treatment of a broad class of constrained monotone inclusion problems. In this general framework, we establish weak convergence of the generated iterates. By introducing a discrete Tikhonov central path, we further prove strong convergence to the minimum-norm solution of the problem under a mild constraint qualification condition on the problem data.

</details>


### [120] [A Multi-Scale Optimization Framework for Grid-Integrated Electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09512)
*Kiernan X. Jennings,Victor M. Zavala,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合整数线性规划（MILP）优化框架，研究碱性水电解槽在短期灵活性需求响应与长期降解驱动的堆栈更换决策之间的多尺度耦合。案例研究显示，该框架可将最优堆栈寿命延长最多2年，生命周期电费降低33%，并挑战了仅通过市场套利实现1美元/千克绿氢成本目标的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电和光伏等可再生能源并网比例不断提高，需要灵活技术来动态转移电力负荷以应对电网波动和紧急事件。水电解槽能通过需求响应提供灵活性的同时实现氢能生产电气化，但高度动态的运行调峰会加速设备降解。现有研究缺乏对短期运行灵活性和长期降解更换决策之间多尺度耦合的量化分析。

Method: 构建了混合整数线性规划（MILP）优化框架，对一台2.2 MW碱性水电解槽在22年时间跨度内的日前市场（DAM）参与进行案例研究，模型耦合了短期运营调度（需求响应）和长期堆栈更换决策（降解驱动）。

Result: 多尺度调度可将最优堆栈寿命延长最多2年，生命周期电费相比不灵活的恒功率运行降低33%。同时量化了关键设备参数权衡和下一代设计目标，分析表明仅通过市场套利难以实现1美元/千克的平准化氢气成本（LCOH）目标。

Conclusion: 该框架量化了电网集成电解槽中多尺度优化尚未被充分挖掘的经济价值，为电解槽在电力市场中的灵活运行和寿命管理提供了决策支持。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of wind and solar resources into the power grid motivates the integration of flexible technologies to dynamically shift power loads in response to grid volatility and emergency events. The water electrolyzer presents a synergistic opportunity to provide flexibility through demand response (DR), while simultaneously electrifying hydrogen production; however, highly dynamic operation schedules accelerate device degradation. This work presents a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) optimization framework to study the multi-scale coupling between short-term operational flexibility provision in electrolysis devices and long-term stack replacement decisions driven by degradation. Active day-ahead market (DAM) participation of a 2.2 MW alkaline water electrolyzer over 22 years is solved as a case study. Our framework reveals that the multi-scale scheduling of DR operation and replacement decisions can extend optimal stack lifetimes by up to 2 years through load-shifting and further reduce lifetime electricity expenses by 33% relative to inflexible constant operation. Furthermore, we quantify key device parameter tradeoffs and next-generation design goals, where our analysis challenges the feasibility of the standard \$1/kg levelized cost of hydrogen (LCOH) production target solely through market arbitrage. Ultimately, this framework quantifies the largely unexploited economic value of multi-scale optimization in grid-integrated electrolysis.

</details>


### [121] [Riemannian Multilevel Optimization with Application to Constrained Energy Minimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09517)
*Yara Elshiaty,Stefania Petra,Jonas Püschel,Tatjana Stykel,Ferdinand-Joseph Vanmaele*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种黎曼流形上的多级优化框架，通过一阶粗网格模型和度量兼容的向量传输算子，在Stiefel、椭球和Bernoulli流形上实现加速收敛，并在Kohn-Sham DFT、Gross-Pitaevskii基态和二元连续割问题上取得显著计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得空间的多级优化方法无法直接应用于流形约束的能量最小化问题，需要将多级优化扩展到黎曼流形上。

Method: 1) 提出与细层目标一阶相容的粗网格模型；2) 在轻度回切凸性假设下保证下降方向；3) 设计度量兼容的向量传输算子（包括内蕴和外蕴两种构造）；4) 基于黎曼Zoutendijk型论证证明全局收敛性。

Result: 在Stiefel流形（Kohn-Sham DFT）、椭球流形（Gross-Pitaevskii基态）和Bernoulli流形（二元连续割）上的实验表明，相比单层黎曼优化，计算时间显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的黎曼多级优化框架能有效加速流形约束的能量最小化问题，且具有全局收敛保证。

Abstract: Multilevel optimization methods are highly effective for discretized energy minimization problems, but their Euclidean formulation does not directly apply to manifold constraints. We introduce a Riemannian extension of multilevel optimization based on a coarse model that is first-order coherent with the fine-level objective and yields descent directions under mild retraction-convexity assumptions. The framework includes metric-compatible vector transfer operators for passing first-order information between levels, covering both intrinsic and extrinsic constructions. We formulate two-level and multilevel algorithms and prove global convergence using a Riemannian Zoutendijk-type argument. Applications to Kohn--Sham density functional theory, Gross--Pitaevskii ground-state computation, and binary continuous cuts demonstrate the method on Stiefel, ellipsoid and Bernoulli manifolds. The experiments show significant reductions in computational time compared with single-level Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [122] [A certified refinement and asymptotic analysis of the Kuznetsov-Sahinidis diameter bound for Lennard-Jones clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09555)
*Guillaume Lecomte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对Kuznetsov和Sahinidis (2025) 关于Lennard-Jones团簇的直径界进行了严格收紧：用已证最小值和子集不等式替代均匀内能估计，通过排列不等式无松弛地验证所有分布。对92个尺寸（N≥38）实现一层改进，渐近界为ρ_KS = N - Θ(√N)，改进增益为Θ(√N)层，并给出了精确渐近常数。但该改进不影响现有确定性求解器，故作为理论注记发表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kuznetsov和Sahinidis (2025) 给出了限制最优Lennard-Jones团簇的直径界，但其逐层界使用了最松的内能估计−C(n,2)。本文旨在通过更精确的估计（利用已证最小值和子集不等式）来收紧这一界，缩小确定性求解器的搜索区域。

Method: 用已证最小值V5*、V6*和K-S子集不等式替换均匀内能估计；在种群分布上最小化逐层界；采用中心递减分布作为候选最小值；使用序列重排不等式实现无松弛地验证所有分布；所有比较在定向舍入算术中重新验证。

Result: 对5≤N≤200，在92个尺寸上实现了对已发表界的一层改进（影响尺寸N≥38）；渐近界ρ_KS = N - Θ(√N)，改进增益为Θ(√N)层，给出了精确渐近常数。下游测试（N≤6）表明直径界并非确定性求解器的瓶颈约束。

Conclusion: 该改进是一个严格的先验界收紧，解决了K-S留下的渐近形式问题，但未解决任何开放全局优化案例，因影响尺寸（N≥38）上尚无求解器运行，故作为理论注记发表。

Abstract: Kuznetsov and Sahinidis (J. Glob. Optim., 2025) prove distance bounds that confine optimal Lennard-Jones clusters and shrink the search region of deterministic solvers; their diameter bound charges each unit-width layer the loosest internal energy $-\binom{n}{2}$. We replace this by a certified estimate built from their own subset inequality and the proven minima $V_5^*$, $V_6^*$, and minimize the resulting layer bound over population profiles. Centred decreasing profiles supply candidate minima; an arrangement-free relaxation, whose only classical ingredient is the rearrangement inequality for sequences, closes every certificate over all profiles; and all comparisons are re-verified in directed-rounding arithmetic. For $5 \le N \le 200$ this certifies a one-layer improvement of the published bound at 92 sizes. The improvement resolves no open global-optimization case: it is a rigorous tightening of a published a priori bound, with a precise account of the mechanism. A direct downstream test on the only tractable sizes ($N \le 6$) finds the diameter box is not the binding resource for a deterministic solver there, and no solver runs at the sizes the refinement affects ($N \ge 38$); we therefore present the result as a theoretical note. We also derive the asymptotic form of the bound, $ρ_{KS} = N - Θ(\sqrt{N})$, resolving a point left open by Kuznetsov and Sahinidis; the gain of the refinement itself grows like $Θ(\sqrt{N})$ layers, with an exact asymptotic constant.

</details>


### [123] [Perturbed utility Markovian traffic equilibrium: theory and computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09568)
*Rui Yao,Kenan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扰动效用马尔可夫均衡（PUME）框架，在保持链路级马尔可夫交通均衡模型可扩展性的同时，将其推广至边界选择概率、非折扣网络加载和一般链路交互场景，并证明了其变分不等式形式的存在唯一性及算法的全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模交通分配需要兼具行为合理性和计算可处理性的均衡模型。现有马尔可夫交通均衡（MTE）模型受限于可加随机效用（ARUM）假设，无法处理边界选择概率（即某些路径流量为零），且难以适应非可分、非对称的成本结构。

Method: 首先提出扰动效用马尔可夫选择模型（PUMCM），通过凸剩余函数定义Bellman最优性算子，其梯度直接给出最优策略；然后构建变分不等式（VI）形式的均衡模型，无需先验选择集限制；最后开发修正策略迭代法进行网络加载，并设计带保护加速元算法求解均衡。

Result: PUME框架在基准网络和合成网络上的实验表明，该方法具有高度可扩展性和鲁棒性，能适应广泛的供需设置；所提出的两种算法均具有全局收敛性和满意的数值表现。

Conclusion: PUME框架统一了行为合理性与计算可处理性，突破了传统ARUM-MTE模型在边界概率和非对称成本结构上的限制，为大规模交通分配提供了一种更灵活、可扩展的均衡建模方案。

Abstract: Large-scale traffic assignment requires equilibrium models that are both behaviorally plausible and computationally tractable. This paper develops a perturbed utility Markovian equilibrium (PUME) framework that preserves the scalability of link-based Markovian traffic equilibrium models and extends their applicability to settings with boundary choice probabilities, undiscounted network loading, and general link interactions. As the behavioral basis of PUME, we first develop the perturbed utility Markovian choice model (PUMCM) in which the Bellman optimality operator is defined through a convex surplus function whose gradient directly yields the optimal policy. The model generalizes existing additive random utility (ARUM) Markovian choice models and admits both interior and boundary choice probabilities. Accordingly, unattractive links can receive zero flow without imposing ex ante choice-set restrictions as in existing ARUM models. We establish conditions under which the corresponding Markov decision problem is well posed and yields a proper demand mapping. We then formulate the equilibrium as a variational inequality (VI) problem on the dual cost space and establish its existence and uniqueness. Particularly, the VI formulation of PUME accommodates non-separable and asymmetric cost structures and thus offers a more flexible modeling framework than existing Markovian traffic equilibrium (MTE) models. For computation, we develop a modified policy iteration method for network loading and a safeguarded accelerated meta-algorithm for computing equilibrium. Both algorithms are proven to be globally convergent and have demonstrated satisfactory numerical performances. Experiments on benchmark and synthetic networks further show that the proposed framework is highly scalable and robust towards a wide variety of demand-supply settings.

</details>
